| 2026-06-12 | **[SESSION 58] ADR-0025 ACCEPTED (T-156): Net semantics for daily-loss accumulation formalized; 28 first-ever direct accumulation tests added** | Net-semantics formalization only — no code changes required; existing `_accumulate_daily_loss()` implementation was already correct. ADR-0025 amends ADR-0012 by specifying: `current_daily_loss = max(0, −daily_net_pnl)` where `daily_net_pnl = Σpnl_i` for all trades closed on a calendar day. Kill switch fires when `current_daily_loss ≥ initial_bankroll × MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT`. Cross-day isolation: `_reset_daily_loss_if_new_day()` zeros both fields on day boundary. Threshold anchored to `initial_bankroll_usd`, not current bankroll (prevents shrinking-threshold feedback loop). Motivation: G3 incident — a profitable high-churn night had gross losses $317.76 vs net P&L −$198.84; gross accumulation would have fired the kill switch on a winning session, defeating the purpose of the control. 28 tests across 4 classes: `TestAccumulateDailyLoss` (11 tests: single loss/profit, net offset/full-cover/excess, additive accumulation, no-touch invariants), `TestResetDailyLossIfNewDay` (7 tests: same-day no-op, new-day reset, missing-key edge case, field isolation), `TestCrossDayTransitions` (3 tests: cross-day isolation, carry-forward suppression), `TestKillSwitchThresholdBoundary` (7 tests: constant value, threshold derivation, below/at/above boundary, net recovery, initial-bankroll anchor). 885/885 tests pass. | DS |

| 2026-05-22 | **[RETROACTIVE / T-154] CHANGE_CONTROL.md framework decisions extracted — three-tier classification, SKELETON→ACTIVE ADR requirement, no-emergency-override rule, conditional-language escalation rule** | T-154 governance hygiene pass. (1) **Three-tier change classification (Class A/B/C):** Adopted alongside ADR-0001 (Compass OS). Class A = ADR required + immutable record; Class B = CHANGELOG entry only; Class C = artifact update only. Rationale: changes that affect capital risk, compliance, or model behavior require the full deliberation artifact (ADR); tactical changes require only a record; administrative changes require only state update. (2) **SKELETON→ACTIVE promotion is Class A (ADR required), not Class B:** A band promotion without an ADR makes a live execution path silently active — equivalent to promoting a model version without ADR-0004 controls. Machine-readable `band_status` field in `BAND_REGISTRY` is what separates SKELETON (code runs, no bets) from ACTIVE (bets fire). An ADR is required because this is a capital-risk-bearing state change, not a documentation change. New strategy registration (no live capital) is Class B because no capital-bearing state change occurs at registration time. (3) **No emergency override path for risk limits:** If a risk limit needs urgent adjustment, the correct response is the kill switch (halt all activity) — not a temporary limit override. An emergency override path that bypasses ADR review would create a governance hole that could be used to rationalize risk increases under pressure. No such path was built and none should be. (4) **Conditional language in canonical registers must be escalated before session end — never deferred:** Conditional language ("may be revised", "subject to DS decision") that survives a context window transition looks like a resolved decision to the next context. This was observed in GG-001: a `# 10 concurrent open (stub)` comment survived four gate clearances. The escalation-before-session-end rule closes this class of failure. | DS |

| 2026-06-22 | **[SESSION 69] DS DECISION — Option C (feature-set diagnosis sprint) + DIAGNOSIS FINDINGS: anti-predictive edge driven by missing playoff-context λ multiplier, not model class failure** | **DS decision (Earl Ignacio, 2026-06-22 S69):** T-177 escalation path = **Option C — feature-set diagnosis sprint.** Not Option A (halt + ADR-0004 supersession) and not Option B (direction inversion — flagged as not trivially safe). Rationale: the anti-predictive edge is identifiable in the data as a specific feature engineering gap, making a model class change premature. T-072 (model retrain) may proceed in Phase 2 but targets the feature fix identified below, not a change of model architecture. **Diagnosis findings (n=103 clean bets, NBA Finals G2–G5, conducted S69):** Full feature-set breakdown run on `data/nba_finals_clean_dataset.csv` (is_clean=True). **Finding 1 — Direction asymmetry.** YES bets (n=36, edge>0): win rate 38.9%. NO bets (n=67, edge≤0): win rate 68.7%. YES/NO split is exact (edge>0 ≡ YES direction in this dataset). **Finding 2 — Model vs market calibration error.** YES bets: mean model_p=0.704, mean mip=0.539, actual win rate=0.389. Model error (model_p − actual): +0.315. Market error (mip − actual): +0.150. Market is 2.1× better calibrated on YES bets. NO bets: mean (1−model_p)=0.825, mean (1−mip)=0.693, actual win rate=0.687. Model error: +0.138. Market error: **+0.006** — Kalshi market is essentially perfectly calibrated on NO bets. **Finding 3 — Threshold amplification.** YES bets by threshold: thr=10 (model_p=0.893, mip=0.724, actual=0.643; model_err=+0.250, mkt_err=+0.081); thr=15 (model_p=0.642, mip=0.476, actual=0.250; model_err=+0.392, mkt_err=+0.226); thr=20 (model_p=0.493, mip=0.346, actual=0.222; model_err=+0.271, mkt_err=+0.123). The model's YES error worsens dramatically at thr≥15; at thr=15-20 both model and market overestimate but model is 2-3× worse. **Finding 4 — Implied λ compression.** Inverting model_p to recover the implied Poisson λ: at thr=10, model implies λ=14.0 but actual_pts=12.8 (compression=0.911); at thr=15, model implies λ=16.1 but actual_pts=12.8 (compression=0.792); at thr=20, model implies λ=19.6 but actual_pts=13.8 (compression=0.703). The model systematically assumes scoring rates ~10-30% higher than Finals reality. **Finding 5 — Within-series compression drift.** YES bet win rates by game: G2=71.4% (avg_pts=17.3), G3=25.0% (avg_pts=11.8), G4=41.7% (avg_pts=15.5), G5=22.2% (avg_pts=7.7). Scoring collapses across the series as teams adjust defensive game plans game-by-game. The model cannot update λ within a series; it uses rolling regular-season averages throughout. **Finding 6 — Market efficiency asymmetry.** Kalshi is almost perfectly calibrated on NO bets (+0.006 error) and significantly better than the model on YES bets. The market appears to incorporate Finals-context defensive information that the model's λ does not. **Root cause identified: missing playoff-context multiplier on λ.** The Poisson survival model uses `opp_adj_lambda` calibrated against regular-season opponent defensive ratings. NBA Finals defensive intensity systematically depresses scoring by 0.70–0.91× regular-season λ, with the compression worsening at higher thresholds and deepening across the series. The model has no feature representing playoff round, within-series opponent familiarity, or Finals-specific defensive adjustments. The Kalshi market has this information via informed participant pricing. **This is a feature engineering gap, NOT a model class failure.** The Poisson architecture is appropriate for basketball scoring; the λ input is wrong. **What this finding does NOT prove:** (a) that the anti-predictive pattern will persist in NBA regular season (where no "Finals compression" exists and rolling averages track reality more closely); (b) that the pattern will appear in MLB (different scoring structure). The n=103 sample is a single series with unusual context — ADR-0024 structural adequacy threshold (n≥500, ≥15 opponents) is not met. **Authorized next steps for feature fix (T-072 scope addition):** (i) Add `playoff_round_multiplier` feature to C2 λ derivation — value 1.0 for regular season; to be calibrated from NBA playoff historical data during Phase 2. (ii) Investigate whether `opp_adj_lambda` uses regular-season defensive ratings for Finals opponents — if so, replace with opponent's most recent N playoff games. (iii) Within-series momentum feature: player's scoring in this series vs regular-season rolling average (captures in-series game-plan adjustment). (iv) All three features must be derived from empirical data during Phase 2 retrain — no guessed multipliers. **GG-037 alpha half + GG-004 status unchanged:** diagnosis identifies the likely mechanism but does not close the governance gap; that requires EXP-017 CONFIRMED_POSITIVE at ADR-0024 adequate sample (n≥500/≥15 opponents). T-177 monitor remains active. The DS decision to pursue Option C means that if EXP-017 (MLB or NBA regular season) fires ANTI-PREDICTIVE again after the feature fix is incorporated, the escalation path defaults to Option A (halt + ADR-0004 review). | DS |

Root cause: pandas chunks large CSVs and emits `DtypeWarning` when metadata columns (`train_test_label`, `matchup`, `opponent_team_abbr`, `game_date_dt` — cols 63/64/66/68) have mixed types across chunks. | 2026-06-23 | **[SESSION 79] T-184 COMPLETE — EXP-017 MLB commercial validation script (2 new files, 99 tests, 1609 total green)** | **Script:** `scripts/run_exp017_mlb.py` (new, standalone — no PYTHONPATH needed). **Test suite:** `tests/test_t184_exp017_mlb.py` (new, 99 tests). **Purpose:** EXP-017 MLB is the formal commercial validation experiment for GG-004. It answers whether the MLB LR+Platt model generates positive fee-net EV against actual Kalshi KXMLBHIT prices at ADR-0024-adequate sample size. GG-004 remains OPEN until this script returns CONFIRMED_POSITIVE at Tier 2 criteria. **Script design:** Standalone analysis script (no import from `prediction_market_bot`; scipy optional with chi-squared fallback). Reads `logs/paper_portfolio_mlb.json` (default) or `--csv path` (batch backtest mode). Deduplication: one position per unique `kalshi_ticker` (earliest entry, consistent with EXP-018 method). **Three analysis layers: (1) EV analysis (EXP-018 pattern):** EV/bet, win rate, total P&L, YES/NO direction split. Realized P&L from `pnl` field in positions. `compute_pnl_unit(direction, mip, vig, won)` — consistent with EXP-018. **(2) ADR-0024 tier checks:** Tier 1 (n ≥ 150): EV/bet < -$0.03 → `DIAGNOSTIC_SPRINT` (model class review per ADR-0004). Tier 2 single (n ≥ 500): `CONFIRMED_POSITIVE` (EV>0, p<0.05) or `CONFIRMED_NEGATIVE` (EV<-$0.03). Tier 2 multi (`--nba-n ≥ 150` AND n ≥ 150): same criteria with multi-sport evidence. **(3) Edge monotonicity / T-177 guard (GG-037):** Fisher's exact (scipy) or chi-squared approximation; edge>0 vs edge≤0 win-rate comparison; ANTI-PREDICTIVE / MONOTONIC / INCONCLUSIVE verdict; tercile table (3 rows by fee_net_edge quantile). **EV verdict hierarchy:** `INCONCLUSIVE` (n < 20) → `DIAGNOSTIC_SPRINT` (Tier 1, EV < -$0.03) → `CONFIRMED_POSITIVE`/`CONFIRMED_NEGATIVE` (Tier 2, p < 0.05) → `DIRECTIONAL_POSITIVE`/`DIRECTIONAL_NEGATIVE` (below Tier 2). **MLB-specific threshold breakdown:** EV and win-rate split by k ∈ {1,2,3} (1+ hits, 2+ hits, 3+ hits). k=1 has highest hit rate; different market dynamics than k=3 — breakdown exposes threshold-level edge signals. **`compute_fee_net_edge(direction, model_p, mip):`** YES: `model_p − mip − 0.07×mip×(1−mip)`; NO: `mip − model_p − 0.07×mip×(1−mip)`. Falls back to pre-computed `fee_net_edge` field if present in position (stored by C4). `model_p` is required; positions without it are skipped. **CI mode exit codes:** 0 = positive/INCONCLUSIVE; 2 = CONFIRMED_NEGATIVE; 3 = DIAGNOSTIC_SPRINT; 4 = insufficient data. **MLB position schema contract** (what the MLB paper trading runner must store): `kalshi_ticker`, `direction`, `mid_price_at_entry`, `vig_at_entry`, `model_p`, `threshold` (1/2/3), `outcome` (WIN/LOSS), `pnl` (USD), `game_date`, `player_name`, optionally `fee_net_edge`, `duplicate_of`. **Data status at time of script completion:** MLB paper trading has NOT yet started (Phase 3 is August–September 2026). This script runs on empty portfolio — returns INCONCLUSIVE as expected. First real run: once MLB paper trading accumulates ≥ 20 positions (MIN_N). Tier 1 check (n ≥ 150) expected by mid-August at 2026 season pace. **`constants verified: `ADR_0024_TIER1_N=150`, `ADR_0024_TIER1_DIAG_EV=-0.03`, `ADR_0024_TIER2_N_SINGLE=500`, `ADR_0024_TIER2_N_MULTI=150`, `ADR_0024_P_THRESHOLD=0.05`, `KALSHI_FEE_RATE=0.07`, `MIN_N=20`. **Test suite (99 tests in 12 classes):** `TestKalshiFee` (7), `TestComputeFeeNetEdge` (6), `TestComputePnlUnit` (6), `TestLoadPortfolio` (8), `TestLoadCsv` (5), `TestDeduplicate` (6), `TestEvStats` (4), `TestThresholdBreakdown` (8), `TestAdr0024TierCheck` (11), `TestEvVerdict` (7), `TestEdgeMonotonicity` (8), `TestTercileTable` (4), `TestFisherPvalue` (3), `TestEndToEnd` (11), `TestConstantsAndSportLabel` (5). **Suite progression: 1510 → 1609/1609 green (+99), 0 failures.** VPS deployed (commit following this session). | EL |
| 2026-06-23 | **[SESSION 78] T-183 COMPLETE — MLB C3 model training infrastructure (2 new files, 77 tests, 1510 total green)** | **Script:** `scripts/train_mlb_model.py` (new). **Test suite:** `tests/test_t183_mlb_c3.py` (new, 77 tests). **Architecture decision: sport-agnostic `ModelForecaster` + MLB-specific training script — no new `MLBForecaster` class.** Rationale: C3 is sport-agnostic at the class level per ADR-0031; only C1/C2 are sport-specific. Adding a new class would violate the ADR-0031 claim that C4–C8 require no changes when adding a sport. The training script is the sport-specific component; `ModelForecaster` loads and runs any artifact with the standard `{"pipeline", "feature_names", "col_medians"}` schema. **Target variable design (`_build_mlb_target`):** MLB KXMLBHIT markets have variable thresholds (1+, 2+, 3+ hits). Each game row is expanded to one row per threshold: `target = (h >= k)` as Int8/NA. `threshold` is stored as a float feature in the LR model — one model scores any threshold at inference time. `h` (raw hit count) lives in `_NON_FEATURE_MLB` to prevent data leakage. **`_NON_FEATURE_MLB` frozenset** excludes: identity/metadata columns (`player_id`, `player_name`, `team_abbreviation`, `game_id`, `game_date`, `season`, `season_type`, `matchup`, `opponent_team_abbr`, `snapshot_id`, `feature_run_id`, `feature_version`, `temporal_cutoff`, `train_test_label`), C1 audit columns (`ingestion_run_id`, `ingested_at`), raw box-score outcomes (`ab`, `h`, `doubles`, `triples`, `hr`, `rbi`, `bb`, `k`, `hbp`, `sf`), training artifacts (`y_target`), and temporal helper (`game_date_dt`). Features `is_home_game`, `threshold`, `ba_l10`, `opp_sp_era_l5`, `park_factor_hits`, etc. are NOT in the set. **Shadow gate (ADR-0009):** No prior MLB model exists → baseline is the class prior (null model: always predicts `mean(y_holdout)`). `brier_null = p * (1 - p)`. Gate: `brier_delta = brier_null - brier_model >= 0.001` AND `ece <= 0.05`. Script exits `sys.exit(2)` on shadow gate failure (without `--force-save`). **`HOLDOUT_CUTOFF = "2026-01-01"` fixed before training:** 2026 MLB season data is the holdout. Anti-gaming split — do not change without a superseding entry. **`MIN_TRAIN_SAMPLES = 30`, `MIN_HOLDOUT_SAMPLES = 10`** (pre-expansion; after 3× threshold expansion: ≥90 train, ≥30 holdout minimum). **sklearn Pipeline** matches `forecaster.py:354-358` exactly: `StandardScaler → CalibratedClassifierCV(LR(C=1.0, solver="lbfgs", max_iter=1000, random_state=42, class_weight="balanced"), method="sigmoid", cv=3)`. **`_mlb_model_version(feature_names)`:** `"mlb-v1-" + sha1[:8]` of `"LogisticRegression+PlattScaling\|MLB\|" + ",".join(sorted(feature_names))` — deterministic, order-independent. **Artifact schema** matches NBA artifact loader contract (`run_pregame.py` loads via `joblib.load`): `{"pipeline": pipe, "feature_names": feature_names, "col_medians": col_medians, "model_version": model_version, "thresholds": [1,2,3], "sport": "mlb"}`. **`CALIBRATION_TEMPERATURE["mlb"] = 1.0` (identity):** Confirmed in `calibration.py`; comment: "No MLB resolved data exists yet; toggled once MLB dark-period dataset available (DS directive 2026-06-16)." T-176 (MLB calibration temperature derivation) remains open. **BOT_C4_ACTIVE guard:** `train_mlb_model.py` exits `sys.exit(1)` if `BOT_C4_ACTIVE` is set — training must never run from within a live execution context. **Test contamination root cause and fix:** 10 of 77 tests in `TestEndToEndDryRun` passed in isolation but failed in the full 1510-test suite with "FATAL: BOT_C4_ACTIVE is set." Root cause: `tests/test_t3_integration.py` imports `from prediction_market_bot.run_paper_trade import T3_CONFIRM_MULTIPLIER, T3_FADE_MULTIPLIER`, which triggers `run_paper_trade.py:59`: `os.environ["BOT_C4_ACTIVE"] = "true"` at module import time — contaminating all downstream tests in the pytest session. Fix: `autouse=True` fixture on `TestEndToEndDryRun` that calls `monkeypatch.delenv("BOT_C4_ACTIVE", raising=False)` before each test in that class. Pattern documented: BOT_C4_ACTIVE guards belong only on scripts that invoke C4 (`run_paper_trade.py`, `run_t4_live.py`); the env-var set-at-import in `run_paper_trade.py` is a side-effect that test isolation must account for. **Test suite (77 tests in 11 classes):** `TestBuildMlbTarget` (13), `TestComputeEce` (5), `TestImpute` (4), `TestMlbModelVersion` (5), `TestExtractFeatureMatrix` (8), `TestNonFeatureSet` (10), `TestNullBaseline` (3), `TestShadowGateLogic` (6), `TestModelArtifact` (4), `TestEndToEndDryRun` (14), `TestMultipleThresholds` (5). **Suite progression: 1433 → 1510/1510 green (+77), 0 failures. No VPS behavior change (train_mlb_model.py is an offline training script; no runner wires it yet).** | EL |
| 2026-06-23 | **[SESSION 77] T-182 COMPLETE — MLB C2 baseball feature set (2 new files, 60 tests, 1433 total green)** | **Module:** `src/prediction_market_bot/c2_feature_store/mlb_features.py` (new). **Test suite:** `tests/test_t182_mlb_c2.py` (new, 60 tests). **C1 patch:** `mlb_ingestion.py` `_ip_to_decimal()` added — MLB Stats API "inningsPitched" uses baseball notation ("5.2" = 5 innings + 2 outs = 5⅔ = 5.667 decimal); this fix prevents a systematic short-outing of pitcher stats. **Feature version:** `MLB_FEATURE_VERSION = "1.0.0"` per ADR-0004 policy (separate from NBA 1.4.0). **TI-01 compliance:** all rolling features computed via `.shift(1).rolling(min_periods=MIN_PERIODS)` — current game box score NEVER enters its own feature vector; `temporal_cutoff = game_date of prior game` (NaT for first game of player log). **Rolling batter features (L10 window, min_periods=3):** `ba_l10`, `slg_l10`, `obp_l10`, `ops_l10`, `h_l10_mean`, `k_rate_l10`, `bb_rate_l10`, `hr_rate_l10`. **Sum-then-divide pattern:** BA/SLG/OBP use Σ(h)/Σ(ab) rolling sums rather than mean of per-game rates — eliminates bias from partial-AB games (a 0-for-1 games receives the same denominator weight as a 4-AB game in the per-game-rate approach). **Context features:** `days_rest` (days since prior game), `b2b_flag` (days_rest < 2). **Park factors:** static 30-venue dict `_PARK_FACTOR_HITS` keyed by `venue_name` string from game_starters; Coors Field=1.120 (extreme hitter's park), Oracle Park=0.970 (pitcher-friendly); unknown venue→default 1.000. **Opponent context:** `opp_runs_allowed_l10` from `MLBTeamStatsStore.get_opp_runs_allowed()` (L10, min_periods=3, TI-01). **Starting pitcher overlay (MANDATORY):** SP identified via `game_starters[game_id]` — home batter gets away SP, away batter gets home SP. SP features: `opp_sp_era_l5`, `opp_sp_k9_l5`, `opp_sp_bb9_l5` from `MLBPitcherStatsStore.get_*_l5(sp_id, game_date)` (L5 starts, min_periods=2, TI-01). When sp_id is None or SP has < 2 prior starts: all SP features=None, `opp_sp_is_known=0.0`. K9/BB9 computed via total K/BB ÷ total IP (same sum-then-divide discipline as batter rates — avoids per-start rate bias from short outings). **Metadata:** `feature_run_id = f"C2MLB-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"`, `feature_version = "1.0.0"`, `train_test_label = None` (populated by `assign_train_test_labels()`). **C6 audit trail:** 5 event types — `mlb_feature_derivation_start`, `mlb_feature_derivation_complete`, `mlb_feature_derivation_error`, `temporal_integrity_warning` (fires when any player has NaT temporal_cutoff), `mlb_train_test_split_applied`. `ok=False` returned and error event logged if C6 logger.log() returns falsy (logger failure path). **Train/test split:** `assign_train_test_labels(feature_df, train_end_date, val_end_date)` — rows with NaT `temporal_cutoff` → `train_test_label=None` (TI-02 excluded from splits); others: ≤train_end→TRAIN, ≤val_end→VALIDATION, else→TEST. **Test suite (60 tests in 5 classes):** `TestIpToDecimal` (11 — full notation coverage including edge cases), `TestParkFactorTable` (4 — extremes, full-range, unknown-venue guard), `TestComputeRollingBatterFeatures` (16 — TI-01 correctness, first/few-game NaN, BA/SLG/OPS/OBP values, days_rest, b2b_flag, window boundary), `TestComputeFeaturesBulk` (23 — IF-C1-C2 contract, SP known/unknown/insufficient, park factor known/unknown, team store, C6 audit, logger failure, feature_run_id prefix, temporal_integrity_warning, train_test_label=None), `TestAssignTrainTestLabels` (5 — chronological split, NaT exclusion, boundary inclusive, audit log). **Key testing lesson:** `pytest.approx()` does not compose correctly with pandas `.all()` — `(series == pytest.approx(value)).all()` fails silently. Fix: use `np.allclose(series.values, value)` for float comparisons, direct equality for exact values. **1373→1433 total tests (+60), 0 failures. No VPS behavior change (C2 is a new module; no runner wires it yet).** | EL |

| 2026-06-24 | **[SESSION 81 cont.] MLB Stats API IP-blocking finding — VPS blocked for historical seasons; generate_mlb_features.py must run locally; PIPE-05 feature refresh design constraint registered** | **Finding (SESSION 81 — T-187B setup):** During T-187B execution on VPS (157.230.91.33 / DigitalOcean), the MLB Stats API (`statsapi.mlb.com`) returned HTTP 406 for all historical season endpoints (2024, 2025) but HTTP 200 for the current season (2026). This is CDN/IP-based access control: DigitalOcean cloud IPs are blocked from accessing historical MLB data. Confirmed via: (1) `GET /api/v1/sports/1/players?season=2024` → 406 from VPS, 200 from local machine. (2) `GET /api/v1/people/{id}/stats?season=2024` → 406 from VPS. (3) Browser User-Agent header makes no difference (406 regardless). (4) 2026 season data: accessible from VPS (HTTP 200). Root cause: `_fetch_position_players(season)` calls `resp.raise_for_status()` which raises on 406, caught by exception handler which returns `[]`; cached empty list survives as bad cache. Players for 2024/2025 were cached as `[]` before issue was discovered. **Resolution:** (1) Killed VPS process. (2) Deleted bad cache files (`players_2024.json`, `players_2025.json`). (3) Generate full 3-season feature matrix locally (all seasons accessible from local machine). (4) SCP output CSV to VPS → run `train_mlb_model.py` on VPS. **Architectural constraint established:** MLB Stats API historical data (season < current) requires local/non-cloud IP to access. VPS can ONLY access current-season data. **PIPE-05 feature refresh design:**  Model training must occur locally (or from an unblocked IP). Daily VPS feature refresh for inference must use `--seasons 2026` only (current season accessible). Implication: `exp_output/MLB/mlb_feature_matrix.csv` on VPS starts as a 3-season locally-generated file; daily VPS refresh with `--seasons 2026` will overwrite it with 2026-only features over time. This is acceptable for inference quality (most-recent features matter for betting); the initial 3-season data is only needed for model training. For annual retraining, local generation + SCP upload must be repeated. **No ADR required:** this is an infrastructure/deployment constraint, not an algorithm decision. Registered as known operational constraint for PIPE-05 runbook. | EL |

| 2026-06-24 | **[SESSION 81 / T-187B] Per-threshold MLB LR+Platt models: pooled model ECE failure root cause, architecture fix, and t=3 exclusion from active scope** | **Root cause of ECE=0.1246 FAIL on pooled model:** A single `CalibratedClassifierCV(LR, class_weight="balanced", cv=3)` trained on all three thresholds pooled together cannot produce well-calibrated per-threshold probabilities. `class_weight="balanced"` forces equal per-class representation across all threshold values, inflating the effective positive rate to ~26% (pooled average). But per-threshold actual rates diverge dramatically: t=1 actual=54.5%, t=2 actual=18.4%, t=3 actual=3.9%. Platt scaling (Logistic sigmoid) fitted on the pooled distribution calibrates to the 26% average and cannot correct per-threshold systematic biases simultaneously. Result: at holdout evaluation, t=1 model predicts 73.3% (actual 54.5%, over-predicts +18.8pp); t=2 model predicts 35.4% (actual 18.4%, over-predicts +17.0pp). ECE = 0.1246 >> 0.05 threshold. **Diagnostic run (local — VPS OOM on full CSV):** Per-threshold Platt ECE verified by running separate pipelines on each threshold's subset. Results: t=1 ECE=0.013 (PASS ≤0.05), t=2 ECE=0.011 (PASS), t=3 ECE=0.004 (PASS). Brier delta: t=1=+0.0055 (PASS ≥0.001), t=2=+0.0022 (PASS), t=3=+0.0002 (FAIL — below threshold). **Architecture fix:** Per-threshold separate `StandardScaler → CalibratedClassifierCV(LR, sigmoid, cv=3)` pipelines for t=1 and t=2. Each pipeline trains on its own threshold's rows only; Platt scaling calibrates to the correct per-threshold base rate. `threshold` is removed from the feature vector — it no longer varies within a per-threshold pipeline and would be a degenerate constant column. `col_medians` are shared (computed from the full `train_df` before splitting by threshold). Shadow gate: BOTH t=1 AND t=2 must PASS for the artifact to be saved. **t=3 exclusion (GG-042 registered):** At t=3, base rate=3.9% and AUC=0.61 produce a Brier improvement of only +0.0002 (required ≥0.001). Even if the gate passed, t=3 bets would almost never fire — the model predicts ~3.9% while Kalshi asks ~8¢ → edge is consistently negative. t=3 is excluded from `THRESHOLDS` pending T-187G (live SP overlay injection, which would provide meaningful signal for rare 3+ hit events). `_EXCLUDED_THRESHOLDS = [3]` registered and GG-042 logged. **Artifact schema change:** Old: `{"pipeline": pipe, ...}`. New: `{"pipelines": {1: pipe1, 2: pipe2}, "feature_names": [...18 features...], "col_medians": medians, "model_version": model_version, "thresholds": [1, 2], "sport": "mlb"}`. `_mlb_model_version(feature_names)` hash unchanged (features identical across thresholds). **Inference change:** `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` dispatches `pipelines[int(mkt.threshold)].predict_proba(X)[0, 1]`. `threshold` removed from `_build_mlb_feature_vector` signature and `_NON_FEATURE_MLB`. `_MODEL_THRESHOLDS = frozenset({1, 2})`. **Test count:** 9 changes in `test_run_mlb_paper_trade.py`, 7 changes in `test_t183_mlb_c3.py`. Shadow gate expected to PASS after re-train on VPS with updated script. | EL |

Adding an MLB equivalent there would couple two sport-specific data sources into a single method, violating the C1/C2 sport-specificity boundary. The clean split: PaperTrader's responsibility is ledger mutation under the GG-034 file-lock invariant; the resolution script's responsibility is fetching the sport-specific stat. `settle_position(position_id, actual_stat, *, stat_label="actual_pts", session_id=None)` is the new interface — it handles threshold comparison, direction-aware P&L formula (`size * (1 - ask) / ask` for wins, `-size` for losses), ledger mutation, and `_accumulate_daily_loss()`, all under `self._lock, self._file_lock()`. `stat_label` is a stored metadata field only — `actual_pts` for NBA backward compat, `actual_hits` for MLB. EXP-017 does not read this field (only `outcome` + `pnl`). **P&L formula:** YES win: `pnl = size * (1 - (mid + vig/2)) / (mid + vig/2)`; YES loss: `pnl = -size`. NO win: `pnl = size * (1 - ((1-mid) + vig/2)) / ((1-mid) + vig/2)`; NO loss: `pnl = -size`. Identical to the formula in `resolve_pending()` — verified by regression tests. **`run_resolve_mlb.py` (PIPE-05R) design decisions:** (1) BOT_ENV=paper guard at module import (sys.exit(1) otherwise). Rate limited at 0.6s/call (mirrors `mlb_ingestion.py`). All exceptions caught silently; returns None on any failure (position stays open for retry). (4) `fetch_actual_hits(player_id, game_date)` tries `game_type="R"` (Regular Season) first, then `game_type="P"` (Postseason) — mirrors NBA `resolve_pending()` Playoffs fallback pattern. (5) Future-date guard: only positions with `game_date < today` are eligible; future-dated positions are skipped silently. (6) API None → C6 warning + position stays open (idempotent: safe to rerun; will retry on next 06:30 run). (7) VPS accessibility caveat documented in docstring: 2026 MLB Stats API accessible from DigitalOcean; historical ≤2025 blocked (Fastly CDN, HTTP 406). **`deploy/cron.conf` naming correction (PIPE-05R → PIPE-05FX):** SESSION 81 added the feature refresh cron at 22:30 UTC with the comment label "PIPE-05R". This was incorrect — PIPE-05R by naming convention belongs to the resolution-only script (matching PIPE-03R = NBA resolution). Feature refresh is not a resolution pipeline; renamed to PIPE-05FX (FX = Feature eXtraction) in this session. PIPE-05R is now correctly assigned to `run_resolve_mlb.py` at 06:30 UTC. The per-player market loop passes `sp_override = sp_override_by_player.get(player_id)` to `_build_mlb_feature_vector`. **SP fetch latency:** ~10 unique SPs on a full MLB game night; each `fetch_pitcher_game_log` call takes ~0.6s (DEFAULT_REQUEST_DELAY_S); total ~6s overhead at PIPE-05 start. | 2026-06-24 | **[SESSION 82] T-187H COMPLETE — t=3 shadow gate re-evaluation: valid negative confirmed; GG-042 structurally deferred** | **Purpose of T-187H:** GG-042 registered t=3 (3+ hits, KXMLBHIT-...-4) as excluded from `_MODEL_THRESHOLDS` pending T-187G (live SP overlay). GG-042 resolution condition: "When `opp_sp_era_l5`, `opp_sp_k9_l5` have well-populated training rows for 3+ hit contexts AND the SP overlay provides discrimination at the t=3 base rate, re-run T-187B with t=3 included." T-187G is now COMPLETE; T-187H is the formal re-evaluation. **Shadow gate re-evaluation (local full 3-season feature matrix, 2026-06-24):** Ran the T-187B per-threshold pipeline independently for t=3 on `exp_output/MLB/mlb_feature_matrix.csv` (126,421 rows; holdout cutoff 2026-01-01). Results: train_n=101,400 rows at t=3; holdout_n=25,021 rows. null_rate=3.91%. Brier delta=**+0.0002 (FAIL** — required ≥0.001). ECE=0.0037 (PASS). AUC=**0.6141**. **t=3 shadow gate FAIL — same result as T-187B.** T-187G did not change the training data and therefore did not change the shadow gate outcome. **Root cause analysis — why the failure is structural:** (1) SP coverage: verified 99.9% of t=3 positive events (h≥3) have `opp_sp_is_known=1`. The GG-042 premise (poor SP coverage causing the failure) was DISPROVED. Coverage is already excellent; T-187G's live overlay addresses inference accuracy, not training data quality. (2) Score distribution analysis: p50=0.037 (≈ base rate). Only 1.9% of holdout predictions exceed 0.08 (≈ Kalshi market price). Even at AUC=0.61, the model cannot shift predictions meaningfully above the market price — it clusters around the 3.9% base rate. (3) Brier delta ceiling at 3.9% base rate: at null_rate=0.039, the maximum possible Brier delta is ~p*(1-p) = 0.037. Any LR model with AUC=0.61 will produce a small absolute delta even with perfect calibration. This is a fundamental information-theoretic limit, not a feature quality issue. (4) Edge would almost never fire: only 487 of 25,021 holdout predictions exceed 0.08 (1.9%) — model_p < market_price_implied = negative edge for 98.1% of bets. **Conclusion:** The t=3 exclusion is structurally correct and not resolvable via SP feature improvement. A richer model (non-linear, additional SP feature interactions, pitcher style matchup data) or different market dynamics (Kalshi price drops below 5–6%) are the only paths to t=3 activation. Neither is available at current system state. **No code changes.** `THRESHOLDS = [1, 2]` and `_EXCLUDED_THRESHOLDS = [3]` in `train_mlb_model.py` unchanged. `_MODEL_THRESHOLDS = frozenset({1, 2})` in `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` and `run_mlb_calib_monitor.py` unchanged. **GG-042 status updated to DEFERRED — structural.** Test suite unchanged at 1790/1790. | EL |

| 2026-06-25 | **[SESSION 84] T-189 COMPLETE — PIPE-05 three-concern remediation: GAME_DATE=tomorrow, market date filter, ensure_persisted(), hydrate fix, PIPE-05FX cache preservation; 29 new tests, 1819 total green** | **Three operational failures surfaced in PIPE-05 session-83 first run (23:31 UTC 2026-06-24) addressed in this session. (1) Contamination guard fires every night.** Root cause: `GAME_DATE = datetime.now(tz=UTC).strftime("%Y%m%d")` — today's date. At 23:31 UTC (7:31pm ET), east-coast MLB games are "Live" → `_is_mlb_game_in_progress(today_iso)` returns True → abort, 0 bets. Fix: `GAME_DATE = (now_utc + timedelta(days=1)).date()` — T1 night-before baseline design. Tomorrow's games are always "Preview" at 23:30 UTC. **Architectural decision:** Market date filter added alongside GAME_DATE fix. Without it, active Kalshi markets for today's in-progress games could pass through the markets_by_player loop even after fixing the contamination guard (Kalshi returns all open markets regardless of date). Filter: `if _ticker_to_game_date(m.ticker) == GAME_DATE_ISO:` ensures only markets for tomorrow's slate are evaluated. (2) `paper_portfolio_mlb.json` missing.** Root cause: `PaperTrader._load_or_init()` only writes to disk on first `_save()` call (triggered by bet placement); contamination guard aborted before any bets, so file was never created. Fix design: adding the disk write to `_load_or_init()` itself was rejected — `_reload_locked()` inside `resolve_pending()` re-reads from disk under the file lock, which would overwrite any direct `_state` mutations (pattern used by 10 unit tests, which all failed). Correct fix: `PaperTrader.ensure_persisted()` — no-op if file exists, calls `_save()` otherwise. Called in `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` immediately after PaperTrader construction, before contamination guard check. | 2026-06-24 | **[SESSION 82] T-187F COMPLETE — MLB calibration monitor (PIPE-05C): derive_temperature design, T_global strategy, non-monotonic miscalibration finding, 35 new tests, 1772 total green** | **Script design: `scripts/run_mlb_calib_monitor.py`.** Reads `paper_portfolio_mlb.json` closed positions; filters to active thresholds `_MODEL_THRESHOLDS = frozenset({1, 2})` (t=3 excluded per GG-042); computes ECE per threshold; derives recommended calibration temperature; reports reliability buckets and anti-predictive check. Does NOT write to `calibration.py` — temperature changes require ADR. Exit codes: 0=CALIBRATED or insufficient data, 1=MISCALIBRATED (ECE>0.05), 2=ANTI_PREDICTIVE. **Temperature derivation (`derive_temperature`): `scipy.optimize.minimize_scalar` on log-loss of `sigmoid(logit(P)/T)`, bounds=(0.3, 8.0).** Mirrors `scripts/derive_calibration.py` exactly. T>1 shrinks (overconfident model); T<1 sharpens (underconfident); T=1 is identity. Gate: `MIN_N_DIAGNOSE=50` positions required for derivation (falls back to identity T=1.0). **T_global strategy (key design decision):** Temperature is derived on the combined all-threshold dataset, then the SAME T_global is applied to both per-threshold ECE diagnoses. This avoids underfitting on per-threshold subsets: at ~50 total positions, each threshold contributes ~25 positions — insufficient for stable Platt fitting. The per-threshold ECE diagnosis answers "does this threshold have a calibration problem at the current T?" not "what is the optimal T for this threshold?". Per-threshold temperature fitting would require ≥50 per-threshold (i.e., ≥100 total) — deferred until data volume justifies it. **ECE formula:** `sum_b (n_b/n) * |mean_pred_b − mean_actual_b|` over 5 reliability buckets [(0.5,0.6),(0.6,0.7),(0.7,0.8),(0.8,0.9),(0.9,1.01)]. ADR-0027 shadow gate threshold ≤0.05. **Anti-predictive check:** calibrated edge = `PC − MIP − 0.07 × MIP × (1 − MIP)`; compares win rate of edge>0 cohort vs edge≤0 cohort. Raises exit code 2 if edge>0 wins at lower rate (mirrors GG-037 diagnosis logic for NBA). **PIPE-05C cron entry:** `10 14 * * 1` — Mondays 14:10 UTC (10 minutes after model freshness check). Exit codes captured in `logs/mlb_calib_monitor.log`. **Key finding during test development: temperature scaling cannot fix non-monotonic miscalibration.** A uniformly overconfident model (all positions predict p=0.90, actual win rate 0.55) IS fully corrected by temperature scaling — T shifts P_cal to match the average win rate, collapsing ECE to near-zero regardless of input model_p. This made naive overconfident test fixtures produce exit code 0 instead of 1. Non-monotonic miscalibration — two groups with inverted calibration errors (Group A severely underconfident, Group B severely overconfident) — CANNOT be fixed by a single multiplicative temperature because the two errors cannot be simultaneously corrected. Test fixtures required: Group A (p=0.51, win_rate=0.85) + Group B (p=0.99, win_rate=0.15). At T=8.0 (max bound): Group A P_cal=0.501 lands in [0.5,0.6) bucket, Group B P_cal=0.640 lands in [0.6,0.7) bucket — different buckets, ECE ≈ 0.43 >> 0.05. All MIP=0.05 ensures all calibrated edges remain positive (anti-predictive check skipped → exit 1 not exit 2). **Test coverage (35 tests in 10 classes):** `TestLoadPositions` (4) — skips duplicates/non-WIN-LOSS/None model_p, reads mid_price_at_entry; `TestToArrays` (6) — directional frame YES/NO P/MIP transforms (uses processed pos dict with "mip" key, not raw JSON "mid_price_at_entry"); `TestDeriveTemperature` (4) — identity at T=1, overconfident >1, underconfident <1, bounds respected; `TestComputeEce` (5) — zero, single bucket, multi-bucket, perfect calibration, boundary 0.9-1.0; `TestDiagnoseThreshold` (4) — insufficient data (exit None/False), sufficient PASS, sufficient FAIL; `TestMainNoFile` (1); `TestMainNoPositions` (1); `TestMainInsufficientData` (2) — deferred at n<50; `TestMainCalibrated` (3) — passes at n≥50 PASS; `TestMainMiscalibrated` (4) — non-monotonic FAIL on combined thresholds; `TestMainAntiPredictive` (1) — edge>0 cohort losing at higher rate. **Suite: 1737 → 1772/1772** (0 failures, 1 skipped). | EL |

| 2026-06-27 | **[SESSION 90 / T-194] `mark_aged_open()` design: in-place tagging within `open_positions`, NOT movement to a separate list** | `PaperTrader.mark_aged_open()` adds `aged_open=True`, `aged_open_reason`, `aged_open_at` fields to the position dict in-place within `self._state["open_positions"]`. It does NOT move the position to `closed_positions` or a separate aged list. **Reason:** `_ledger_bankroll()` derives `open_cost` by summing `open_positions[*]["size_usd"]`. Moving a position out of `open_positions` would credit that capital back to the bankroll — incorrect, because the money is still committed (the paper bet exists; we just cannot resolve it). This would overstate available capital and allow subsequent over-sizing. **Boundary condition (>48h rule):** Age is computed from midnight of `game_date` in UTC (not game start time, which is unavailable at resolve time). Only positions with `game_date < today` are in the resolution loop; an additional `age_hours > 48` check against datetime.now(UTC) ensures the midnight-boundary case cannot trigger early. Fixed-noon UTC in boundary tests prevents test failures at the exact midnight transition. **C6 behavior:** `mark_aged_open()` emits `paper_position_aged_open` WARNING to C7 audit log. The position remains in `open_positions` and is excluded from future resolution attempts (caller responsibility — runner skips `aged_open=True` positions). | EL |
| 2026-06-27 | **[SESSION 90 / T-195] Permissive-with-logging pattern for silent exception handlers in MLB contamination guards** | Two functions had bare `except Exception: pass`: `_is_mlb_game_in_progress()` in `run_mlb_paper_trade.py` and `_fetch_live_game_pks()` in `run_t2_mlb_sameday.py`. **Design decision:** Both are intentionally PERMISSIVE on failure (return `False` / empty set = assume no games in progress = allow pipeline to proceed). This is correct for a contamination guard: false-negative (treating a live game as not-live) is the safe fail mode. A false-positive (treating a non-live schedule as live) would block all bets unnecessarily. **Only change:** Added optional `logger: AuditLogger | None = None` and `session_id: str | None = None` parameters. When logger is provided, API failures emit `mlb_schedule_api_failed` WARNING to C6 instead of swallowing silently. The permissive return behavior is unchanged. **Why not raise?** Raising would convert an API transient failure into a pipeline abort, blocking all bets. The guard's purpose is to prevent stale positions during live games — if the schedule API is down, the conservative path (allow bets) is correct, but the failure should be visible in the audit log. | EL |
